The tech giant's threat intelligence division said the adversarial targeting of the sector is centered around four key themes: striking defense entities deploying technologies on the battlefield in the Russia-Ukraine War, directly approaching employees and exploitation of the hiring process by North Korean and Iranian actors, use of edge devices and appliances as initial access pathways for China-nexus groups, and supply chain risk stemming from the breach of the manufacturing sector. <input type='checkbox' name='article_514' value='https://thehackernews.com/2026/02/uat-9921-deploys-voidlink-malware-to.html' /> 514 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=thehackernews.com+UAT-9921+Deploys+VoidLink+Malware+to+Target+Technology+and+Financial+Sectors%EE%A0%82Feb+13%2C+2026Cloud+Security+%2F+Cyber+EspionageA+previously+unknown+threat+actor+tracked+as+UAT-9921++has+been+observed+leveraging+a+new+modular+framework+called+VoidLink+in+its+campaigns+targeting+the+technology+and+financial+services+sectors%2C+according+to+findings+from+Cisco+Talos.++%22This+threat+actor+seems+to+have+been+active+since+2019%2C+although+they+have+not+necessarily+used+VoidLink+over+the+duration+of+their+activity%2C%22+researchers+Nick+Biasini%2C+Aaron+Boyd%2C+Asheer+Malhotra%2C+and+Vitor+Ventura+said+.+%22UAT-9921+uses+compromised+hosts+to+install+VoidLink+command-and-control+%28C2%29%2C+which+are+then+used+to+launch+scanning+activities+both+internal+and+external+to+the+network.%22++VoidLink+was+first+documented++by+Check+Point+last+month%2C+describing+it+as+a+feature-rich+malware+framework+written+in+Zig+designed+for+long-term%2C+stealthy+access+to+Linux-based+cloud+environments.+It%27s+assessed+to+be+the+work+of+a+single+developer+with+assistance+from+a+large+language+model+%28LLM%29+to+flesh+out+its+internals+based+on+a+p...' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://thehackernews.com/2026/02/uat-9921-deploys-voidlink-malware-to.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UAT-9921 Deploys VoidLink Malware to Target Technology and Financial SectorsFeb 13, 2026Cloud Security / Cyber EspionageA previously unknown threat actor tracked as UAT-9921  has been observed leveraging a new modular framework called VoidLink in its campaigns targeting the technology and financial services sectors, according to findings from Cisco Talos.  "This threat actor seems to have been active since 2019, although they have not necessarily used VoidLink over the duration of their activity," researchers Nick Biasini, Aaron Boyd, Asheer Malhotra, and Vitor Ventura said . "UAT-9921 uses compromised hosts to install VoidLink command-and-control (C2), which are then used to launch scanning activities both internal and external to the network."  VoidLink was first documented  by Check Point last month, describing it as a feature-rich malware framework written in Zig designed for long-term, stealthy access to Linux-based cloud environments.
